15:["$","main",null,{"className":"flex-1 overflow-y-auto","children":["$","div",null,{"className":"mx-auto max-w-4xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 xl:max-w-6xl","children":["$","$L16",null,{"data":{"date":"2026-10-13","generated_at":"2026-10-17T07:27:18.450139+00:00","stats":{"total":151,"starred":1,"must_read":13,"skim":77,"glance":61,"by_category":{"pretraining_data":5,"llm_pretrain":3,"scaling":1,"architecture":15,"efficient":21,"eval":17,"multimodal":18,"multimodal_gen":7,"post_train":27,"mechanism":17,"coding_agent":3,"agent":17},"fetch_status":{"arxiv":"ok","huggingface":"ok"}},"categories":[{"id":"pretraining_data","emoji":"📚","name":"预训练数据","description":"以预训练数据为核心贡献的工作：数据采集/清洗/去重、数据配比(data mixture)、数据筛选/质量评估、语料构建、数据课程(data curriculum)、数据重复(repetition)、合成数据用于预训练、Tokenizer/词表设计、成员推断/数据检测。判据是『论文核心是数据本身』，而非训练 recipe/优化器(→ llm_pretrain)或评测资产(→ eval)","papers":[{"id":"2610.13321","title":"Host-Aware Web Crawling for LLM Pretraining Data","abstract":"Web crawling is a major source of large language model (LLM) pretraining data, yet data-selection pipelines discard a large share of crawled pages. We investigate how a crawler should allocate its budget across and within hosts to collect more high-quality pretraining data. Combining document-level quality classifier scores, web-graph centrality metrics, and Common Crawl's per-host crawl coverage, we find that roughly half of the variation in classifier scores occurs between hosts. In the crawls","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-12","category_id":"pretraining_data","cover_url":"/covers/2610_13321.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"LLM 预训练网页抓取成本很高，但后续数据筛选会丢掉大量页面；问题在于 crawler 应如何在 host 间和 host 内分配预算，才能更高效地收集高质量预训练数据。","importance":"这是很贴近基座训练现实的数据管线工作。它不讨论后验过滤，而是把问题前移到 crawl allocation：在抓取阶段就减少无效页面。对大规模语料构建、预算分配、host-level quality prior 都有直接启发。","technical_approach":"作者结合文档级质量分类器分数、web-graph centrality 指标以及 Common Crawl 的 host 覆盖信息，分析质量方差在 host 间与 host 内的分布。核心发现是：约一半质量分数方差来自 host 间差异；对一个 host 先抽样少量页面，就能较好估计其剩余页面质量。基于此，论文模拟了不同 crawl allocation 策略，比较 host 选择与 host 深度抓取的收益。","experiments":"实验基于已有 crawl 数据和模拟分配策略，而非训练完整 LLM。结果显示：用 harmonic centrality 决定抓哪些 host，相比随机排序收益有限，对某些质量分类器甚至更差；而“先少量采样、再对高分 host 加深抓取”的策略能在相同预算下收集到更多高质量页面。摘要未给出具体增益数字，但方向很清楚：host-aware adaptive depth 比单纯 centrality 更有效。","authors_note":"作者为 Malte Ostendorff。摘要可确认其工作聚焦 Common Crawl 风格网页抓取与预训练数据质量分析。"},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Web crawling for LLM pretraining is expensive, yet downstream filtering discards a large fraction of pages. The paper asks how crawl budget should be allocated across and within hosts to collect more high-quality pretraining data.","importance":"This is directly relevant to foundation-model data pipelines. Instead of only improving post-hoc filtering, it moves the optimization upstream to crawl allocation, which matters for large-scale corpus construction under fixed bandwidth and storage budgets.","technical_approach":"The paper combines document-level quality classifier scores, web-graph centrality metrics, and Common Crawl host coverage statistics to analyze where quality variation comes from. Roughly half of the variation is between hosts, and a small sample from a host is informative about the quality of its remaining pages. This depends on difficulty relative to the current policy and per-problem sampling budget, not on absolute model scale. With 100× compute, stronger policies shift previously learnable items toward ‘always succeed’ while pulling harder items from ‘always fail’ into the learnable band—so an adaptive difficulty calibration mechanism remains necessary. Recursive synthesis acts as a distribution controller that keeps data in the learnable regime, a scale-stable interface property that is unlikely to be washed out or rank-reversed by larger models."},"plan":{"goal":{"zh":"验证：在等 RL 计算下，难度校准合成是否显著提升 RLVR 的有效梯度信号与最终 Pass@k；并验证“成功率分桶→学习贡献”是否可预测。","en":"Validate whether difficulty-calibrated synthesis increases effective RLVR gradient signal and final Pass@k under iso-RL compute, and whether success-rate buckets predict learning contribution."},"data_sources":{"zh":"Proxy 模型 150M / 350M / 1.3B 三档（同 tokenizer/上下文长度），同一数学可验证集合（如 GSM8K-style + 自建可验证算术/代数模板）。每档固定 rollout budget（如每题 N=16 或 32），并固定 reward/grader（程序化判定或严格答案匹配）。","en":"Three proxy scales: 150M / 350M / 1.3B (same tokenizer/context). Run on-policy rollouts on an existing RLVR set, estimate per-problem success rate p̂, bucket by p̂, and record per-bucket advantage variance, token KL, and reward-improvement slope per unit compute."},{"zh":"步骤 2：干预对照（等计算）。(A) 仅过滤：保留中间桶样本；(B) 递归合成/改写：对落在两端桶的题目做自动变体生成与难度调参（增加/减少步骤数、引入干扰项、改变数值范围/组合结构），直到在当前策略下 p̂ 落入目标区间（如 0.2–0.8）。在相同总 rollout 数与相同更新步数下训练，对比最终 Pass@1/Pass@k 与学习曲线斜率，并在三档模型上复现趋势。","en":"Step 2: Iso-compute intervention.
